# Magic Patterns alignment log

The manifest contains 20 screens and 87 HTML states. Each state is a page for this review, giving a target of 8,700 entries. Shared component changes are recorded for each state where the rendered result changed. The gallery is the review index.

The first two passes restore the light-theme page background and default text. Chromium measured both computed values on every state at the prototype viewport of 1024 × 768. The temporary activity placeholder inherits the app theme. Its layout has no dedicated prototype frame.

The [Pibbo App](https://www.magicpatterns.com/os/eDDkMMZM4QuKmuCTaj4fkB?file=kEXWdPBhS1LJVpJoSCC6YH) and [Pibbo Brand](https://www.magicpatterns.com/os/eDDkMMZM4QuKmuCTaj4fkB?file=4aFNgJX6NFacCvnSiaCvwt) have separate stylesheets.
